  1223	    private fun drawMockInputDimOverlayAndActiveControl(
  1224	        canvas: Canvas,
  1225	        stageLeft: Float,
  1226	        stageTop: Float,
  1227	        stageScale: Float,
  1228	        alpha: Float,
  1229	        yOffset: Float,
  1230	        focusShiftY: Float
  1231	    ) {
  1232	        val dimProgress = mockInputDimProgress.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1233	        if (dimProgress <= 0.01f) return
  1234	
  1235	        inputDimPaint.alpha = (dimProgress * MOCK_INPUT_DIM_ALPHA * 255f).roundToInt()
  1236	        canvas.drawRect(0f, 0f, width.toFloat(), height.toFloat(), inputDimPaint)
  1237	        inputDimPaint.alpha = 255
  1238	
  1239	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * 26f).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  1240	        formFieldStrokePaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * 185f).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  1241	        formFieldStrokePaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_STROKE_WIDTH * stageScale
  1242	        formCaretPaint.strokeWidth = 1.45f * stageScale
  1243	
  1244	        val activeDropdown = expandedMockDropdown ?: mockInputDimActiveDropdown
  1245	        if (activeDropdown != null && activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) {
  1246	            drawMockDropdownControl(
  1247	                canvas,
  1248	                activeDropdown,
  1249	                mockDropdownX(activeDropdown),
  1250	                mockDropdownY(activeDropdown, yOffset, focusShiftY),
  1251	                stageLeft,
  1252	                stageTop,
  1253	                stageScale,
  1254	                alpha,
  1255	                mockDropdownWidth(activeDropdown)
  1256	            )
  1257	            if (expandedMockDropdown == activeDropdown) {
  1258	                drawMockDropdownOptions(canvas, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1259	            }
  1260	            return
  1261	        }
  1262	
  1263	        val activeField = focusedMockField ?: mockInputDimActiveField ?: return
  1264	        drawMockSignInField(
  1265	            canvas,
  1266	            activeField,
  1267	            mockActiveFieldX(activeField),
  1268	            mockActiveFieldY(activeField, yOffset, focusShiftY),
  1269	            stageLeft,
  1270	            stageTop,
  1271	            stageScale,
  1272	            alpha,
  1273	            mockActiveFieldWidth(activeField)
  1274	        )
  1275	    }

  1277	    private fun drawMockSignInTitle(
  1278	        canvas: Canvas,
  1279	        x: Float,
  1280	        fieldTopY: Float,
  1281	        stageLeft: Float,
  1282	        stageTop: Float,
  1283	        stageScale: Float,
  1284	        alpha: Float
  1285	    ) {
  1286	        val motion = alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1287	        val sourceTextSize = signInAccountPromptTextSize() * stageScale
  1288	        val targetTextSize = MOCK_FORM_TITLE_TEXT_SIZE * stageScale
  1289	        val sourceCenterX = stageLeft + ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_CENTER_X * stageScale
  1290	        val targetCenterX = stageLeft + (x + MOCK_FORM_WIDTH / 2f) * stageScale
  1291	        val sourceCenterY = stageTop +
  1292	            (ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CENTER_Y + signInStackShiftY() + signInAccountPromptExtraShiftY()) * stageScale
  1293	        formTitlePaint.textSize = sourceTextSize
  1294	        val sourceMetrics = formTitlePaint.fontMetrics
  1295	        val sourceBaselineY = sourceCenterY - (sourceMetrics.ascent + sourceMetrics.descent) / 2f
  1296	        val targetBaselineY = stageTop + (fieldTopY - MOCK_FORM_TITLE_BASELINE_GAP) * stageScale
  1297	
  1298	        formTitlePaint.textSize = lerpFloat(sourceTextSize, targetTextSize, motion)
  1299	        formTitlePaint.alpha = 255
  1300	        formTitlePaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1301	        canvas.drawText(
  1302	            ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_TEXT,
  1303	            lerpFloat(sourceCenterX, targetCenterX, motion),
  1304	            lerpFloat(sourceBaselineY, targetBaselineY, motion),
  1305	            formTitlePaint
  1306	        )
  1307	    }

  1309	    private fun drawMockCreateAccountTitle(
  1310	        canvas: Canvas,
  1311	        x: Float,
  1312	        fieldTopY: Float,
  1313	        stageLeft: Float,
  1314	        stageTop: Float,
  1315	        stageScale: Float,
  1316	        alpha: Float
  1317	    ) {
  1318	        val motion = alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1319	        val sourceTextSize = signInAccountPromptTextSize() * stageScale
  1320	        val targetTextSize = MOCK_FORM_TITLE_TEXT_SIZE * stageScale
  1321	        val sourceX = stageLeft + ACCOUNT_PROMPT_LEFT_ANCHOR_X * stageScale
  1322	        val targetX = stageLeft + x * stageScale
  1323	        val sourceCenterY = stageTop +
  1324	            (ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CENTER_Y + signInStackShiftY() + signInAccountPromptExtraShiftY()) * stageScale
  1325	        formTitlePaint.textSize = sourceTextSize
  1326	        val sourceMetrics = formTitlePaint.fontMetrics
  1327	        val sourceBaselineY = sourceCenterY - (sourceMetrics.ascent + sourceMetrics.descent) / 2f
  1328	        val targetBaselineY = stageTop + (fieldTopY - MOCK_FORM_TITLE_BASELINE_GAP) * stageScale
  1329	
  1330	        formTitlePaint.textSize = lerpFloat(sourceTextSize, targetTextSize, motion)
  1331	        formTitlePaint.alpha = 255
  1332	        formTitlePaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.LEFT
  1333	        canvas.drawText(
  1334	            ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CREATE_TEXT,
  1335	            lerpFloat(sourceX, targetX, motion),
  1336	            lerpFloat(sourceBaselineY, targetBaselineY, motion),
  1337	            formTitlePaint
  1338	        )
  1339	    }

  1461	    private fun drawMockForgotRecoveryOption(
  1462	        canvas: Canvas,
  1463	        target: MockForgotRecoveryTarget,
  1464	        left: Float,
  1465	        top: Float,
  1466	        stageScale: Float,
  1467	        alpha: Float
  1468	    ) {
  1469	        val right = left + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SELECTOR_BOX_WIDTH * stageScale
  1470	        val bottom = top + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SELECTOR_BOX_HEIGHT * stageScale
  1471	        tempRect.set(left, top, right, bottom)
  1472	        val selected = forgotRecoveryTarget == target
  1473	        val pressed = pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == target
  1474	        val fillAlpha = (alpha * when {
  1475	            pressed -> 62f
  1476	            selected -> 46f
  1477	            else -> 18f
  1478	        }).roundToInt()
  1479	        val strokeAlpha = (alpha * if (selected || pressed) 220f else 168f).roundToInt()
  1480	        val radius = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_RADIUS * stageScale
  1481	
  1482	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb(fillAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
  1483	        formFieldStrokePaint.color = Color.argb(strokeAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
  1484	        formFieldStrokePaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_STROKE_WIDTH * stageScale
  1485	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  1486	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldStrokePaint)
  1487	
  1488	        formInputPaint.textSize = MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SELECTOR_TEXT_SIZE * stageScale
  1489	        formInputPaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  1490	        formInputPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1491	        val metrics = formInputPaint.fontMetrics
  1492	        canvas.drawText(
  1493	            target.displayText,
  1494	            tempRect.centerX(),
  1495	            tempRect.centerY() - (metrics.ascent + metrics.descent) / 2f,
  1496	            formInputPaint
  1497	        )
  1498	    }

  1500	    private fun drawMockFormParagraph(
  1501	        canvas: Canvas,
  1502	        text: String,
  1503	        centerX: Float,
  1504	        topY: Float,
  1505	        maxWidth: Float,
  1506	        stageLeft: Float,
  1507	        stageTop: Float,
  1508	        stageScale: Float,
  1509	        alpha: Float,
  1510	        textSize: Float,
  1511	        lineHeight: Float
  1512	    ): Float {
  1513	        formInputPaint.textSize = textSize * stageScale
  1514	        formInputPaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 218f).roundToInt()
  1515	        formInputPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1516	
  1517	        val lines = wrappedTextLines(text, maxWidth * stageScale, formInputPaint)
  1518	        val metrics = formInputPaint.fontMetrics
  1519	        val x = stageLeft + centerX * stageScale
  1520	        var baselineY = stageTop + topY * stageScale - metrics.ascent
  1521	        lines.forEach { line ->
  1522	            canvas.drawText(line, x, baselineY, formInputPaint)
  1523	            baselineY += lineHeight * stageScale
  1524	        }
  1525	        return topY + lines.size * lineHeight
  1526	    }

  1528	    private fun drawMockCreateAccountAvatarText(
  1529	        canvas: Canvas,
  1530	        columnX: Float,
  1531	        firstFieldY: Float,
  1532	        stageLeft: Float,
  1533	        stageTop: Float,
  1534	        stageScale: Float,
  1535	        alpha: Float
  1536	    ) {
  1537	        val avatarScale = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_STACK_SCALE
  1538	        val scaledAvatarSize = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_SIZE * avatarScale
  1539	        val labelBaselineFromTop = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_LABEL_BASELINE_FROM_TOP * avatarScale
  1540	        val avatarTopGap = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_TOP_GAP * avatarScale
  1541	        val centerX = stageLeft + (columnX + MOCK_FORM_WIDTH / 2f) * stageScale
  1542	        val baselineY = stageTop + (firstFieldY + labelBaselineFromTop) * stageScale
  1543	        formLabelPaint.textSize = MOCK_FORM_LABEL_TEXT_SIZE * avatarScale * stageScale
  1544	        formLabelPaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  1545	        formLabelPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1546	        canvas.drawText(
  1547	            MOCK_FORM_AVATAR_TEXT,
  1548	            centerX,
  1549	            baselineY,
  1550	            formLabelPaint
  1551	        )
  1552	
  1553	        val avatarLeftX = columnX + (MOCK_FORM_WIDTH - scaledAvatarSize) / 2f
  1554	        val avatarTopY = firstFieldY + labelBaselineFromTop + avatarTopGap
  1555	        val avatarBounds = Bounds(
  1556	            avatarLeftX,
  1557	            avatarTopY,
  1558	            scaledAvatarSize,
  1559	            scaledAvatarSize
  1560	        )
  1561	        val carouselProgress = mockCreateAvatarCarouselProgress()
  1562	        val slideDirection = mockCreateAvatarCarouselDirection.stageDirection
  1563	        val slideDistance = mockCreateAvatarSlideDistance(scaledAvatarSize)
  1564	        val dragDirection = mockCreateAvatarDragDirection()
  1565	        val dragProgress = mockCreateAvatarDragProgress(slideDistance, stageScale)
  1566	
  1567	        drawMockCreateAvatarBubbleShell(canvas, avatarBounds, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1568	
  1569	        canvas.save()
  1570	        clipMockCreateAvatarBubbleInterior(canvas, avatarBounds, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale)
  1571	        if (
  1572	            activeMockCreateAvatarDrag &&
  1573	            mockCreateAvatarDragMoved &&
  1574	            dragDirection != null &&
  1575	            !isMockCreateAvatarCarouselAnimating()
  1576	        ) {
  1577	            val dragSlideDirection = dragDirection.stageDirection
  1578	            drawMockCreateAvatarBitmap(
  1579	                canvas,
  1580	                mockCreateAvatarIndex,
  1581	                avatarBounds,
  1582	                -dragSlideDirection * dragProgress * slideDistance,
  1583	                stageLeft,
  1584	                stageTop,
  1585	                stageScale,
  1586	                alpha
  1587	            )
  1588	            drawMockCreateAvatarBitmap(
  1589	                canvas,
  1590	                mockCreateAvatarIndexForDirection(dragDirection),
  1591	                avatarBounds,
  1592	                dragSlideDirection * (1f - dragProgress) * slideDistance,
  1593	                stageLeft,
  1594	                stageTop,
  1595	                stageScale,
  1596	                alpha
  1597	            )
  1598	        } else if (isMockCreateAvatarCarouselAnimating()) {
  1599	            drawMockCreateAvatarBitmap(
  1600	                canvas,
  1601	                mockCreateAvatarCarouselFromIndex,
  1602	                avatarBounds,
  1603	                -slideDirection * carouselProgress * slideDistance,
  1604	                stageLeft,
  1605	                stageTop,
  1606	                stageScale,
  1607	                alpha
  1608	            )
  1609	            drawMockCreateAvatarBitmap(
  1610	                canvas,
  1611	                mockCreateAvatarCarouselToIndex,
  1612	                avatarBounds,
  1613	                slideDirection * (1f - carouselProgress) * slideDistance,
  1614	                stageLeft,
  1615	                stageTop,
  1616	                stageScale,
  1617	                alpha
  1618	            )
  1619	        } else {
  1620	            drawMockCreateAvatarBitmap(
  1621	                canvas,
  1622	                mockCreateAvatarIndex,
  1623	                avatarBounds,
  1624	                0f,
  1625	                stageLeft,
  1626	                stageTop,
  1627	                stageScale,
  1628	                alpha
  1629	            )
  1630	        }
  1631	        canvas.restore()
  1632	        drawMockCreateAvatarChevron(
  1633	            canvas,
  1634	            MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Previous,
  1635	            avatarBounds,
  1636	            stageLeft,
  1637	            stageTop,
  1638	            stageScale,
  1639	            alpha
  1640	        )
  1641	        drawMockCreateAvatarChevron(
  1642	            canvas,
  1643	            MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Next,
  1644	            avatarBounds,
  1645	            stageLeft,
  1646	            stageTop,
  1647	            stageScale,
  1648	            alpha
  1649	        )
  1650	    }

  1692	    private fun clipMockCreateAvatarBubbleInterior(
  1693	        canvas: Canvas,
  1694	        avatarBounds: Bounds,
  1695	        stageLeft: Float,
  1696	        stageTop: Float,
  1697	        stageScale: Float
  1698	    ) {
  1699	        tempPath.reset()
  1700	        tempPath.addCircle(
  1701	            stageLeft + (avatarBounds.x + avatarBounds.w / 2f) * stageScale,
  1702	            stageTop + (avatarBounds.y + avatarBounds.h / 2f) * stageScale,
  1703	            mockCreateAvatarBubbleInnerRadius(avatarBounds, stageScale),
  1704	            Path.Direction.CW
  1705	        )
  1706	        canvas.clipPath(tempPath)
  1707	    }

  1709	    private fun mockCreateAvatarBubbleInnerRadius(avatarBounds: Bounds, stageScale: Float): Float {
  1710	        return (avatarBounds.w / 2f - avatarBounds.w * MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_BUBBLE_INNER_INSET_RATIO) * stageScale
  1711	    }

  1713	    private fun drawMockCreateAvatarChevron(
  1714	        canvas: Canvas,
  1715	        direction: MockAvatarCarouselDirection,
  1716	        avatarBounds: Bounds,
  1717	        stageLeft: Float,
  1718	        stageTop: Float,
  1719	        stageScale: Float,
  1720	        alpha: Float
  1721	    ) {
  1722	        val centerX = stageLeft + mockCreateAvatarChevronCenterX(direction, avatarBounds) * stageScale
  1723	        val centerY = stageTop + mockCreateAvatarChevronCenterY(avatarBounds) * stageScale
  1724	        val halfWidth = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_WIDTH * stageScale / 2f
  1725	        val halfHeight = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_HEIGHT * stageScale / 2f
  1726	        val pressedAlpha = if (pressedMockCreateAvatarNav == direction) 0.62f else 0.9f
  1727	        backChevronPaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * pressedAlpha * 255f).roundToInt()
  1728	        backChevronPaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_STROKE_WIDTH * stageScale
  1729	
  1730	        if (direction == MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Previous) {
  1731	            canvas.drawLine(centerX + halfWidth, centerY - halfHeight, centerX - halfWidth, centerY, backChevronPaint)
  1732	            canvas.drawLine(centerX - halfWidth, centerY, centerX + halfWidth, centerY + halfHeight, backChevronPaint)
  1733	        } else {
  1734	            canvas.drawLine(centerX - halfWidth, centerY - halfHeight, centerX + halfWidth, centerY, backChevronPaint)
  1735	            canvas.drawLine(centerX + halfWidth, centerY, centerX - halfWidth, centerY + halfHeight, backChevronPaint)
  1736	        }
  1737	        backChevronPaint.alpha = 255
  1738	    }

  1740	    private fun drawMockDropdownControl(
  1741	        canvas: Canvas,
  1742	        dropdown: MockDropdown,
  1743	        x: Float,
  1744	        y: Float,
  1745	        stageLeft: Float,
  1746	        stageTop: Float,
  1747	        stageScale: Float,
  1748	        alpha: Float,
  1749	        dropdownWidth: Float = MOCK_FORM_WIDTH
  1750	    ) {
  1751	        tempRect.set(
  1752	            stageLeft + x * stageScale,
  1753	            stageTop + y * stageScale,
  1754	            stageLeft + (x + dropdownWidth) * stageScale,
  1755	            stageTop + (y + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT) * stageScale
  1756	        )
  1757	        val radius = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_RADIUS * stageScale
  1758	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  1759	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldStrokePaint)
  1760	
  1761	        val textAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  1762	        formInputPaint.textSize = mockDropdownTextSize(dropdown) * stageScale
  1763	        formInputPaint.alpha = textAlpha
  1764	        formInputPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.LEFT
  1765	        val textInset = mockDropdownTextInsetX(dropdown) * stageScale
  1766	        val chevronInset = mockDropdownChevronInsetX(dropdown) * stageScale
  1767	        val maxTextWidth = (dropdownWidth * stageScale - textInset - chevronInset * 2f).coerceAtLeast(0f)
  1768	        canvas.drawText(
  1769	            trailingFittingText(mockDropdownDisplayText(dropdown), maxTextWidth, formInputPaint),
  1770	            tempRect.left + textInset,
  1771	            tempRect.centerY() - (formInputPaint.fontMetrics.ascent + formInputPaint.fontMetrics.descent) / 2f,
  1772	            formInputPaint
  1773	        )
  1774	
  1775	        val centerX = tempRect.right - chevronInset
  1776	        val centerY = tempRect.centerY() + 2f * stageScale
  1777	        val halfSize = mockDropdownChevronSize(dropdown) * stageScale / 2f
  1778	        backChevronPaint.alpha = textAlpha
  1779	        backChevronPaint.strokeWidth = 1.65f * stageScale
  1780	        canvas.drawLine(centerX - halfSize, centerY - halfSize / 2f, centerX, centerY + halfSize / 2f, backChevronPaint)
  1781	        canvas.drawLine(centerX, centerY + halfSize / 2f, centerX + halfSize, centerY - halfSize / 2f, backChevronPaint)
  1782	        backChevronPaint.alpha = 255
  1783	    }

  1785	    private fun drawMockDropdownOptions(
  1786	        canvas: Canvas,
  1787	        stageLeft: Float,
  1788	        stageTop: Float,
  1789	        stageScale: Float,
  1790	        alpha: Float
  1791	    ) {
  1792	        val dropdown = expandedMockDropdown ?: return
  1793	        if (activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) return
  1794	
  1795	        val x = mockDropdownX(dropdown)
  1796	        val y = mockDropdownOptionsY(
  1797	            dropdown,
  1798	            MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - alpha),
  1799	            mockFormFocusShiftY(FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress))
  1800	        )
  1801	        val left = stageLeft + x * stageScale
  1802	        val top = stageTop + y * stageScale
  1803	        val right = stageLeft + (x + mockDropdownWidth(dropdown)) * stageScale
  1804	        val optionHeight = mockDropdownOptionHeight(dropdown) * stageScale
  1805	        val optionAlpha = 255
  1806	        val optionTextInset = mockDropdownOptionTextInsetX(dropdown) * stageScale
  1807	        val scrollbarReservedWidth = if (mockDropdownCanScroll(dropdown)) {
  1808	            (MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_WIDTH + MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_INSET_X * 2f) * stageScale
  1809	        } else {
  1810	            0f
  1811	        }
  1812	        val maxTextWidth = (right - left - optionTextInset - scrollbarReservedWidth).coerceAtLeast(0f)
  1813	
  1814	        val visibleOptions = mockDropdownVisibleOptions(dropdown)
  1815	        visibleOptions.forEachIndexed { index, option ->
  1816	            val optionTop = top + index * optionHeight
  1817	            val optionBottom = optionTop + optionHeight
  1818	            val fillColor = if (option == mockDropdownSelectedValue(dropdown)) {
  1819	                Color.argb(optionAlpha, 76, 12, 94)
  1820	            } else {
  1821	                Color.argb(optionAlpha, 14, 2, 18)
  1822	            }
  1823	            tempRect.set(left, optionTop, right, optionBottom)
  1824	            formFieldFillPaint.color = fillColor
  1825	            formFieldStrokePaint.color = Color.argb(optionAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
  1826	            formFieldStrokePaint.strokeWidth = 1.2f * stageScale
  1827	            canvas.drawRect(tempRect, formFieldFillPaint)
  1828	            canvas.drawRect(tempRect, formFieldStrokePaint)
  1829	
  1830	            formInputPaint.textSize = mockDropdownOptionTextSize(dropdown) * stageScale
  1831	            formInputPaint.alpha = optionAlpha
  1832	            formInputPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.LEFT
  1833	            val metrics = formInputPaint.fontMetrics
  1834	            canvas.drawText(
  1835	                trailingFittingText(option, maxTextWidth, formInputPaint),
  1836	                left + optionTextInset,
  1837	                (optionTop + optionBottom) / 2f - (metrics.ascent + metrics.descent) / 2f,
  1838	                formInputPaint
  1839	            )
  1840	        }
  1841	
  1842	        if (mockDropdownCanScroll(dropdown)) {
  1843	            drawMockDropdownScrollbar(
  1844	                canvas,
  1845	                dropdown,
  1846	                top,
  1847	                right,
  1848	                optionHeight * visibleOptions.size,
  1849	                stageScale,
  1850	                optionAlpha
  1851	            )
  1852	        }
  1853	    }
  1854	
  1855	    private fun drawMockDropdownScrollbar(
  1856	        canvas: Canvas,
  1857	        dropdown: MockDropdown,
  1858	        top: Float,
  1859	        right: Float,
  1860	        dropdownHeight: Float,
  1861	        stageScale: Float,
  1862	        alpha: Int
  1863	    ) {
  1864	        val options = mockDropdownOptions(dropdown)
  1865	        val visibleCount = mockDropdownVisibleOptionCount(dropdown)
  1866	        val maxOffset = mockDropdownMaxScrollOffset(dropdown)
  1867	        if (options.isEmpty() || maxOffset <= 0) return
  1868	
  1869	        val trackRight = right - MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_INSET_X * stageScale
  1870	        val trackLeft = trackRight - MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_WIDTH * stageScale
  1871	        val trackTop = top + MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_INSET_Y * stageScale
  1872	        val trackBottom = top + dropdownHeight - MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_INSET_Y * stageScale
  1873	        val trackHeight = (trackBottom - trackTop).coerceAtLeast(0f)
  1874	        val thumbHeight = maxOf(14f * stageScale, trackHeight * visibleCount / options.size)
  1875	            .coerceAtMost(trackHeight)
  1876	        val thumbProgress = mockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown) / maxOffset.toFloat()
  1877	        val thumbTop = trackTop + (trackHeight - thumbHeight) * thumbProgress
  1878	        val radius = MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_WIDTH * stageScale / 2f
  1879	
  1880	        tempRect.set(trackLeft, trackTop, trackRight, trackBottom)
  1881	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * 0.28f).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  1882	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  1883	
  1884	        tempRect.set(trackLeft, thumbTop, trackRight, thumbTop + thumbHeight)
  1885	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * 0.78f).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  1886	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  1887	    }
  1888	
  1889	    private fun drawMockSignInField(
  1890	        canvas: Canvas,
  1891	        field: MockSignInField,
  1892	        x: Float,
  1893	        y: Float,
  1894	        stageLeft: Float,
  1895	        stageTop: Float,
  1896	        stageScale: Float,
  1897	        alpha: Float,
  1898	        fieldWidth: Float = MOCK_FORM_WIDTH
  1899	    ) {
  1900	        tempRect.set(
  1901	            stageLeft + x * stageScale,
  1902	            stageTop + y * stageScale,
  1903	            stageLeft + (x + fieldWidth) * stageScale,
  1904	            stageTop + (y + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT) * stageScale
  1905	        )
  1906	        val radius = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_RADIUS * stageScale
  1907	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  1908	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldStrokePaint)
  1909	
  1910	        val label = mockSignInFieldLabel(field)
  1911	        val labelProgress = mockSignInFieldLabelProgress(field)
  1912	        val textAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  1913	        formLabelPaint.textSize = lerpFloat(
  1914	            MOCK_FORM_LABEL_TEXT_SIZE,
  1915	            MOCK_FORM_LABEL_TEXT_SIZE * MOCK_FORM_FLOATING_LABEL_SCALE,
  1916	            labelProgress
  1917	        ) * stageScale
  1918	        formLabelPaint.alpha = textAlpha
  1919	        formLabelPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.LEFT
  1920	        val metrics = formLabelPaint.fontMetrics
  1921	        val placeholderBaselineY = tempRect.centerY() - (metrics.ascent + metrics.descent) / 2f
  1922	        val floatingBaselineY = tempRect.top + MOCK_FORM_LABEL_FLOAT_TOP_INSET_Y * stageScale - metrics.ascent
  1923	        val labelX = lerpFloat(
  1924	            tempRect.left + MOCK_FORM_LABEL_INSET_X * stageScale,
  1925	            tempRect.left + MOCK_FORM_LABEL_FLOAT_INSET_X * stageScale,
  1926	            labelProgress
  1927	        )
  1928	        val labelBaselineY = lerpFloat(placeholderBaselineY, floatingBaselineY, labelProgress)
  1929	        canvas.drawText(
  1930	            label,
  1931	            labelX,
  1932	            labelBaselineY,
  1933	            formLabelPaint
  1934	        )
  1935	
  1936	        val fieldText = mockSignInFieldDisplayText(field)
  1937	        val shouldDrawInput = fieldText.isNotEmpty() || focusedMockField == field
  1938	        if (shouldDrawInput) {
  1939	            val inputAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * labelProgress * 255f).roundToInt()
  1940	            formInputPaint.textSize = MOCK_FORM_LABEL_TEXT_SIZE * stageScale
  1941	            formInputPaint.alpha = inputAlpha
  1942	            formCaretPaint.alpha = inputAlpha
  1943	
  1944	            val inputX = tempRect.left + MOCK_FORM_LABEL_INSET_X * stageScale
  1945	            val inputBaselineY = tempRect.top + MOCK_FORM_INPUT_BASELINE_FROM_TOP * stageScale
  1946	            val maxInputWidth = fieldWidth * stageScale - MOCK_FORM_LABEL_INSET_X * stageScale * 2f
  1947	            val visibleText = trailingFittingText(fieldText, maxInputWidth, formInputPaint)
  1948	            canvas.drawText(visibleText, inputX, inputBaselineY, formInputPaint)
  1949	
  1950	            if (focusedMockField == field) {
  1951	                val caretX = inputX + formInputPaint.measureText(visibleText) + 2f * stageScale
  1952	                val inputMetrics = formInputPaint.fontMetrics
  1953	                canvas.drawLine(
  1954	                    caretX,
  1955	                    inputBaselineY + inputMetrics.ascent,
  1956	                    caretX,
  1957	                    inputBaselineY + inputMetrics.descent,
  1958	                    formCaretPaint
  1959	                )
  1960	            }
  1961	        }
  1962	    }

  1964	    private fun drawRememberMeCheckbox(
  1965	        canvas: Canvas,
  1966	        x: Float,
  1967	        y: Float,
  1968	        stageLeft: Float,
  1969	        stageTop: Float,
  1970	        stageScale: Float,
  1971	        alpha: Float
  1972	    ) {
  1973	        val paintAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  1974	        val boxLeft = stageLeft + x * stageScale
  1975	        val boxTop = stageTop + y * stageScale
  1976	        val boxRight = boxLeft + MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_BOX_SIZE * stageScale
  1977	        val boxBottom = boxTop + MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_BOX_SIZE * stageScale
  1978	        val fillAlpha = (alpha * if (rememberMeChecked || pressedRememberMe) 46f else 18f).roundToInt()
  1979	        val strokeAlpha = (alpha * if (rememberMeChecked) 220f else 168f).roundToInt()
  1980	
  1981	        rememberMeBoxFillPaint.color = Color.argb(fillAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
  1982	        rememberMeBoxStrokePaint.color = Color.argb(strokeAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
  1983	        rememberMeBoxStrokePaint.strokeWidth = 1.7f * stageScale
  1984	        tempRect.set(boxLeft, boxTop, boxRight, boxBottom)
  1985	        val radius = MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_BOX_RADIUS * stageScale
  1986	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, rememberMeBoxFillPaint)
  1987	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, rememberMeBoxStrokePaint)
  1988	
  1989	        if (rememberMeChecked) {
  1990	            rememberMeCheckPaint.alpha = paintAlpha
  1991	            rememberMeCheckPaint.strokeWidth = 2.45f * stageScale
  1992	            canvas.drawLine(
  1993	                boxLeft + 5.3f * stageScale,
  1994	                boxTop + 11.8f * stageScale,
  1995	                boxLeft + 9.1f * stageScale,
  1996	                boxTop + 15.8f * stageScale,
  1997	                rememberMeCheckPaint
  1998	            )
  1999	            canvas.drawLine(
  2000	                boxLeft + 9.1f * stageScale,
  2001	                boxTop + 15.8f * stageScale,
  2002	                boxLeft + 17.2f * stageScale,
  2003	                boxTop + 6.9f * stageScale,
  2004	                rememberMeCheckPaint
  2005	            )
  2006	            rememberMeCheckPaint.alpha = 255
  2007	        }
  2008	
  2009	        rememberMeTextPaint.textSize = MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_TEXT_SIZE * stageScale
  2010	        rememberMeTextPaint.alpha = paintAlpha
  2011	        rememberMeTextPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.LEFT
  2012	        val metrics = rememberMeTextPaint.fontMetrics
  2013	        val baselineY = (boxTop + boxBottom) / 2f - (metrics.ascent + metrics.descent) / 2f
  2014	        canvas.drawText(
  2015	            MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_TEXT,
  2016	            boxRight + MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_LABEL_GAP * stageScale,
  2017	            baselineY,
  2018	            rememberMeTextPaint
  2019	        )
  2020	        rememberMeTextPaint.alpha = 255
  2021	    }

  2023	    private fun drawBackChevron(
  2024	        canvas: Canvas,
  2025	        centerX: Float,
  2026	        topY: Float,
  2027	        stageLeft: Float,
  2028	        stageTop: Float,
  2029	        stageScale: Float,
  2030	        alpha: Float
  2031	    ) {
  2032	        val chevronAlpha = alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * if (pressedBackChevron) 0.68f else 0.92f
  2033	        val center = stageLeft + centerX * stageScale
  2034	        val top = stageTop + topY * stageScale
  2035	        val bottom = top + MOCK_BACK_CHEVRON_HEIGHT * stageScale
  2036	        val halfWidth = MOCK_BACK_CHEVRON_WIDTH * stageScale / 2f
  2037	
  2038	        backChevronPaint.alpha = (chevronAlpha * 255f).roundToInt()
  2039	        backChevronPaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_BACK_CHEVRON_STROKE_WIDTH * stageScale
  2040	        canvas.drawLine(center - halfWidth, top, center, bottom, backChevronPaint)
  2041	        canvas.drawLine(center, bottom, center + halfWidth, top, backChevronPaint)
  2042	        backChevronPaint.alpha = 255
  2043	    }

  2045	    private fun decode(resId: Int): Bitmap {
  2046	        return BitmapFactory.decodeResource(resources, resId, bitmapOptions)
  2047	    }
  2048	
  2049	    private fun createAvatarCharacterBitmap(
  2050	        source: Bitmap,
  2051	        includeDarkSaturatedPixels: Boolean = false
  2052	    ): Bitmap {
  2053	        val bitmapWidth = source.width
  2054	        val bitmapHeight = source.height
  2055	        val pixels = IntArray(bitmapWidth * bitmapHeight)
  2056	        source.getPixels(pixels, 0, bitmapWidth, 0, 0, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight)
  2057	
  2058	        val mask = createAvatarCharacterMask(pixels, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight, includeDarkSaturatedPixels)
  2059	        val outputPixels = IntArray(pixels.size) { index ->
  2060	            if (mask[index]) pixels[index] else Color.TRANSPARENT
  2061	        }
  2062	        return Bitmap.createBitmap(bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight, Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888).apply {
  2063	            setPixels(outputPixels, 0, bitmapWidth, 0, 0, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight)
  2064	        }
  2065	    }
  2066	
  2067	    private fun createAvatarBubbleShellBitmap(source: Bitmap): Bitmap {
  2068	        val bitmapWidth = source.width
  2069	        val bitmapHeight = source.height
  2070	        val pixels = IntArray(bitmapWidth * bitmapHeight)
  2071	        source.getPixels(pixels, 0, bitmapWidth, 0, 0, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight)
  2072	
  2073	        val centerX = (bitmapWidth - 1f) / 2f
  2074	        val centerY = (bitmapHeight - 1f) / 2f
  2075	        val innerRadius = min(bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight) * (0.5f - MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_BUBBLE_INNER_INSET_RATIO)
  2076	        val innerRadiusSquared = innerRadius * innerRadius
  2077	
  2078	        val outputPixels = IntArray(pixels.size) { index ->
  2079	            val x = index % bitmapWidth
  2080	            val y = index / bitmapWidth
  2081	            val dx = x - centerX
  2082	            val dy = y - centerY
  2083	            if (dx * dx + dy * dy < innerRadiusSquared) {
  2084	                Color.TRANSPARENT
  2085	            } else {
  2086	                pixels[index]
  2087	            }
  2088	        }
  2089	        return Bitmap.createBitmap(bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight, Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888).apply {
  2090	            setPixels(outputPixels, 0, bitmapWidth, 0, 0, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight)
  2091	        }
  2092	    }
  2093	
  2094	    private fun createAvatarCharacterMask(
  2095	        pixels: IntArray,
  2096	        bitmapWidth: Int,
  2097	        bitmapHeight: Int,
  2098	        includeDarkSaturatedPixels: Boolean
  2099	    ): BooleanArray {
  2100	        val coloredPixels = BooleanArray(pixels.size)
  2101	        pixels.forEachIndexed { index, pixel ->
  2102	            coloredPixels[index] = isColorfulAvatarPixel(pixel, includeDarkSaturatedPixels)
  2103	        }
  2104	
  2105	        val mask = BooleanArray(pixels.size)
  2106	        val outlineRadius = 7
  2107	        for (y in 0 until bitmapHeight) {
  2108	            for (x in 0 until bitmapWidth) {
  2109	                val index = y * bitmapWidth + x
  2110	                if (!coloredPixels[index]) continue
  2111	
  2112	                mask[index] = true
  2113	                for (dy in -outlineRadius..outlineRadius) {
  2114	                    val sampleY = y + dy
  2115	                    if (sampleY !in 0 until bitmapHeight) continue
  2116	                    for (dx in -outlineRadius..outlineRadius) {
  2117	                        val sampleX = x + dx
  2118	                        if (sampleX !in 0 until bitmapWidth) continue
  2119	                        val sampleIndex = sampleY * bitmapWidth + sampleX
  2120	                        val samplePixel = pixels[sampleIndex]
  2121	                        val sampleAlpha = Color.alpha(samplePixel)
  2122	                        val sampleMax = maxOf(
  2123	                            Color.red(samplePixel),
  2124	                            Color.green(samplePixel),
  2125	                            Color.blue(samplePixel)
  2126	                        )
  2127	                        if (sampleAlpha > 16 && sampleMax <= 44) {
  2128	                            mask[sampleIndex] = true
  2129	                        }
  2130	                    }
  2131	                }
  2132	            }
  2133	        }
  2134	        return mask
  2135	    }
  2136	
  2137	    private fun isColorfulAvatarPixel(
  2138	        pixel: Int,
  2139	        includeDarkSaturatedPixels: Boolean
  2140	    ): Boolean {
  2141	        val alpha = Color.alpha(pixel)
  2142	        val red = Color.red(pixel)
  2143	        val green = Color.green(pixel)
  2144	        val blue = Color.blue(pixel)
  2145	        val channelMax = maxOf(red, green, blue)
  2146	        val channelMin = minOf(red, green, blue)
  2147	        if (alpha <= 16) return false
  2148	
  2149	        val chroma = channelMax - channelMin
  2150	        return (channelMax > 48 && chroma > 22) ||
  2151	            (includeDarkSaturatedPixels && channelMax > 36 && chroma > 14)
  2152	    }
  2153	
  2154	    private fun standardSignInBackgroundForSize(): Bitmap? {
  2155	        return when {
  2156	            isSizeCloseTo(width, STANDARD_TABLET_LONG_EDGE) &&
  2157	                isSizeCloseTo(height, STANDARD_TABLET_SHORT_EDGE) -> standardLandscapeBackground
  2158	            isSizeCloseTo(width, STANDARD_TABLET_SHORT_EDGE) &&
  2159	                isSizeCloseTo(height, STANDARD_TABLET_LONG_EDGE) -> standardPortraitBackground
  2160	            else -> null
  2161	        }
  2162	    }
  2163	
  2164	    private fun isSizeCloseTo(actual: Int, expected: Int): Boolean {
  2165	        return abs(actual - expected) <= STANDARD_TABLET_SIZE_TOLERANCE
  2166	    }

  2168	    private fun isFinalFrameSettled(): Boolean {
  2169	        return bootProgressAt(bootStartMillis) >= 1f
  2170	    }

  2172	    private fun settledAvatarHitProfile(x: Float, y: Float): CinerificProfile? {
  2173	        if (mockSignInStartMillis != null) return null
  2174	        if (!isFinalFrameSettled()) return null
  2175	
  2176	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return null
  2177	
  2178	        val stackShiftY = signInStackShiftY()
  2179	        return FINAL_AVATAR_TARGETS.firstOrNull { target ->
  2180	            val bounds = target.bounds
  2181	            val centerX = stage.left + (bounds.x + bounds.w / 2f) * stage.scale
  2182	            val centerY = stage.top + (bounds.y + stackShiftY + bounds.h / 2f) * stage.scale
  2183	            val radius = min(bounds.w, bounds.h) * stage.scale / 2f
  2184	            val dx = x - centerX
  2185	            val dy = y - centerY
  2186	            dx * dx + dy * dy <= radius * radius
  2187	        }?.profile
  2188	    }

  2192

  2195	        val centerY = stage.top +
  2196	            (ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CENTER_Y + signInStackShiftY() + signInAccountPromptExtraShiftY()) * stage.scale
  2197	        val right = left + ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CREATE_HIT_WIDTH * stage.scale
  2198	        val halfHeight = ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CREATE_HIT_HEIGHT * stage.scale / 2f

  2200	    }

  2287	    private fun mockCreateAvatarNavHit(x: Float, y: Float): MockAvatarCarouselDirection? {
  2288	        if (mockSignInStartMillis == null || activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) return null
  2289	
  2290	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return null
  2291	        val progress = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockSignInProgress())
  2292	        val yOffset = MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - progress)
  2293	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress))
  2294	        val columnX = mockCreateFormX() + (MOCK_CREATE_FORM_COLUMNS - 1) * (MOCK_FORM_WIDTH + MOCK_CREATE_FORM_COLUMN_GAP)
  2295	        val firstFieldY = MOCK_FORM_Y + yOffset + focusShiftY
  2296	        val avatarBounds = mockCreateAvatarBounds(columnX, firstFieldY)
  2297	        return MockAvatarCarouselDirection.values().firstOrNull { direction ->
  2298	            mockCreateAvatarChevronContains(x, y, direction, avatarBounds, stage)
  2299	        }
  2300	    }

  2303	        if (mockSignInStartMillis == null || activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) return false
  2304

  2307	        val yOffset = MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - progress)
  2308	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress))

  2320	    private fun updateMockCreateAvatarDrag(event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
  2321	        if (!activeMockCreateAvatarDrag) return false
  2322	
  2323	        mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = event.x - mockTouchDownX
  2324	        val dragDeltaY = event.y - mockTouchDownY
  2325	        if (
  2326	            !mockCreateAvatarDragMoved &&
  2327	            abs(mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX) > touchSlop &&
  2328	            abs(mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX) > abs(dragDeltaY)
  2329	        ) {
  2330	            mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = true
  2331	            pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
  2332	        }
  2333	
  2334	        if (mockCreateAvatarDragMoved) {
  2335	            postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  2336	        }
  2337	        return true
  2338	    }

  2340	    private fun mockDropdownOptionHit(pointerX: Float, pointerY: Float): MockDropdownOption? {
  2341	        if (
  2342	            mockSignInStartMillis == null ||
  2343	            activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount ||
  2344	            expandedMockDropdown == null
  2345	        ) {
  2346	            return null
  2347	        }
  2348	
  2349	        val dropdown = expandedMockDropdown ?: return null
  2350	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return null
  2351	        val progress = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockSignInProgress())
  2352	        val yOffset = MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - progress)
  2353	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress))
  2354	        val dropdownX = mockDropdownX(dropdown)
  2355	        val yStart = mockDropdownOptionsY(dropdown, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  2356	        val left = stage.left + dropdownX * stage.scale
  2357	        val right = stage.left + (dropdownX + mockDropdownWidth(dropdown)) * stage.scale
  2358	        if (pointerX !in left..right) return null
  2359	        if (mockDropdownCanScroll(dropdown)) {
  2360	            val scrollbarLeft = right -
  2361	                (MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_WIDTH + MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_INSET_X * 2f) * stage.scale
  2362	            if (pointerX >= scrollbarLeft) return null
  2363	        }
  2364	
  2365	        mockDropdownVisibleOptions(dropdown).forEachIndexed { optionIndex, option ->
  2366	            val optionHeight = mockDropdownOptionHeight(dropdown)
  2367	            val top = stage.top + (yStart + optionIndex * optionHeight) * stage.scale
  2368	            val bottom = top + optionHeight * stage.scale
  2369	            if (pointerY in top..bottom) return MockDropdownOption(dropdown, option)
  2370	        }
  2371	        return null
  2372	    }

  2374	    private fun mockDropdownOptionsHit(pointerX: Float, pointerY: Float): MockDropdown? {
  2375	        if (
  2376	            mockSignInStartMillis == null ||
  2377	            activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount ||
  2378	            expandedMockDropdown == null
  2379	        ) {
  2380	            return null
  2381	        }
  2382	
  2383	        val dropdown = expandedMockDropdown ?: return null
  2384	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return null
  2385	        val progress = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockSignInProgress())
  2386	        val yOffset = MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - progress)
  2387	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress))
  2388	        val dropdownX = mockDropdownX(dropdown)
  2389	        val yStart = mockDropdownOptionsY(dropdown, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  2390	        val left = stage.left + dropdownX * stage.scale
  2391	        val right = stage.left + (dropdownX + mockDropdownWidth(dropdown)) * stage.scale
  2392	        val top = stage.top + yStart * stage.scale
  2393	        val bottom = top + mockDropdownVisibleOptionCount(dropdown) * mockDropdownOptionHeight(dropdown) * stage.scale
  2394	        return if (pointerX in left..right && pointerY in top..bottom) dropdown else null
  2395	    }

  2397	    private fun updateMockDropdownScroll(event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
  2398	        val dropdown = activeMockDropdownScroll ?: return false
  2399	        if (expandedMockDropdown != dropdown || !mockDropdownCanScroll(dropdown)) return false
  2400	
  2401	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return true
  2402	        val optionHeightPx = (mockDropdownOptionHeight(dropdown) * stage.scale).coerceAtLeast(1f)
  2403	        val dragDeltaY = event.y - mockDropdownScrollStartY
  2404	        if (abs(dragDeltaY) > touchSlop) {
  2405	            mockDropdownScrollMoved = true
  2406	            pressedMockDropdownOption = null
  2407	        }
  2408	
  2409	        if (mockDropdownScrollMoved) {
  2410	            val rowDelta = ((mockDropdownScrollStartY - event.y) / optionHeightPx).roundToInt()
  2411	            setMockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown, mockDropdownScrollStartOffset + rowDelta)
  2412	            postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  2413	        }
  2414	        return true
  2415	    }

  2417	    private fun mockBackChevronHit(x: Float, y: Float): Boolean {
  2418	        if (mockSignInStartMillis == null || isMockSignInClosing()) return false
  2419	
  2420	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return false
  2421	        val progress = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockSignInProgress())
  2422	        val yOffset = MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - progress)
  2423	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress))
  2424	        val chevronTopY = when (activeMockFlow) {
  2425	            MockAccountFlow.SignIn -> {
  2426	                val lastField = activeMockFields().lastOrNull() ?: return false
  2427	                val lastFieldY = mockActiveFieldY(lastField, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  2428	                lastFieldY + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT + MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_TOP_GAP + MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_BOX_SIZE +
  2429	                    MOCK_BACK_CHEVRON_TOP_GAP
  2430	            }
  2431	            MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount -> {
  2432	                MOCK_FORM_Y + yOffset + focusShiftY + mockCreateFormHeight() + MOCK_BACK_CHEVRON_TOP_GAP
  2433	            }
  2434	            MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword -> mockForgotPasswordBackChevronTopY(yOffset, focusShiftY)
  2435	            null -> return false
  2436	        }
  2437	        return mockBackChevronContains(x, y, mockActiveFormCenterX(), chevronTopY, stage)
  2438	    }
  2439	
  2440	    private fun shouldStartMockDragReturn(event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
  2441	        if (mockDragReturnInProgress || isMockSignInClosing()) return false
  2442	
  2443	        val upwardDragY = mockTouchDownY - event.y
  2444	        if (upwardDragY <= mockBackDragThresholdPx()) return false
  2445	
  2446	        val dragX = event.x - mockTouchDownX
  2447	        return upwardDragY > abs(dragX)
  2448	    }
  2449	
  2450	    private fun mockBackDragThresholdPx(): Float {
  2451	        val stageThreshold = currentStageMetrics()?.let { stage ->
  2452	            MOCK_BACK_DRAG_THRESHOLD * stage.scale
  2453	        } ?: MOCK_BACK_DRAG_THRESHOLD
  2454	        return maxOf(stageThreshold, touchSlop * 3f)
  2455	    }

  2457	    private fun mockSignInFieldContains(
  2458	        pointerX: Float,

  2460	        fieldX: Float,

  2462	        stage: StageMetrics,
  2463	        fieldWidth: Float = MOCK_FORM_WIDTH
  2464	    ): Boolean {

  2466	        val top = stage.top + fieldY * stage.scale
  2467	        val right = stage.left + (fieldX + fieldWidth) * stage.scale
  2468	        val bottom = stage.top + (fieldY + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT) * stage.scale
  2469	        return pointerX in left..right && pointerY in top..bottom
  2470	    }

  2481	        val right = stage.left + (x + MOCK_FORM_WIDTH) * stage.scale
  2482	        val bottom = stage.top + (y - MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_HIT_TOP_PADDING + MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_HIT_HEIGHT) * stage.scale

  2486	    private fun mockForgotRecoveryTargetContains(

  2496	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SELECTOR_LABEL_GAP +

  2498	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SELECTOR_BOX_GAP
  2499	        val rowLeft = mockForgotPasswordFormX() + MOCK_FORM_WIDTH / 2f - rowWidth / 2f
  2500	        val firstBoxX = rowLeft + labelWidth + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SELECTOR_LABEL_GAP
  2501	        val optionIndex = if (target == MockForgotRecoveryTarget.Username) 0f else 1f
  2502	        val boxX = firstBoxX +
  2503	            optionIndex * (MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SELECTOR_BOX_WIDTH + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SELECTOR_BOX_GAP)
  2504	        val left = stage.left + boxX * stage.scale
  2505	        val top = stage.top + selectorY * stage.scale
  2506	        val right = stage.left + (boxX + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SELECTOR_BOX_WIDTH) * stage.scale
  2507	        val bottom = stage.top + (selectorY + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SELECTOR_BOX_HEIGHT) * stage.scale
  2508	        return pointerX in left..right && pointerY in top..bottom
  2509	    }

  2527	    private fun mockCreateAvatarBounds(columnX: Float, firstFieldY: Float): Bounds {

  2529	        return Bounds(
  2530	            columnX + (MOCK_FORM_WIDTH - scaledAvatarSize) / 2f,
  2531	            firstFieldY +
  2532	                MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_LABEL_BASELINE_FROM_TOP * MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_STACK_SCALE +
  2533	                MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_TOP_GAP * MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_STACK_SCALE,
  2534	            scaledAvatarSize,
  2535	            scaledAvatarSize
  2536	        )
  2537	    }

  2539	    private fun mockCreateAvatarCarouselBounds(
  2540	        columnX: Float,
  2541	        firstFieldY: Float,
  2542	        avatarBounds: Bounds
  2543	    ): Bounds {
  2544	        val centerX = columnX + MOCK_FORM_WIDTH / 2f
  2545	        val contentTop = firstFieldY
  2546	        val contentBottom = avatarBounds.y + avatarBounds.h
  2547	        val contentCenterY = (contentTop + contentBottom) / 2f
  2548	        val chevronSpan = avatarBounds.w +
  2549	            (MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_SIDE_GAP + MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_HIT_WIDTH / 2f) * 2f
  2550	        val side = maxOf(chevronSpan, contentBottom - contentTop)
  2551	        return Bounds(
  2552	            centerX - side / 2f,
  2553	            contentCenterY - side / 2f,
  2554	            side,
  2555	            side
  2556	        )
  2557	    }

  2563	    private fun mockCreateAvatarChevronCenterX(
  2564	        direction: MockAvatarCarouselDirection,
  2565	        avatarBounds: Bounds
  2566	    ): Float {
  2567	        return if (direction == MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Previous) {
  2568	            avatarBounds.x - MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_SIDE_GAP
  2569	        } else {
  2570	            avatarBounds.x + avatarBounds.w + MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_SIDE_GAP
  2571	        }
  2572	    }

  2576	    }

  2578	    private fun mockCreateAvatarChevronContains(
  2579	        pointerX: Float,
  2580	        pointerY: Float,
  2581	        direction: MockAvatarCarouselDirection,
  2582	        avatarBounds: Bounds,
  2583	        stage: StageMetrics
  2584	    ): Boolean {
  2585	        val centerX = stage.left + mockCreateAvatarChevronCenterX(direction, avatarBounds) * stage.scale
  2586	        val centerY = stage.top + mockCreateAvatarChevronCenterY(avatarBounds) * stage.scale
  2587	        val halfWidth = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_HIT_WIDTH * stage.scale / 2f
  2588	        val halfHeight = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_HIT_HEIGHT * stage.scale / 2f
  2589	        return pointerX in (centerX - halfWidth)..(centerX + halfWidth) &&
  2590	            pointerY in (centerY - halfHeight)..(centerY + halfHeight)
  2591	    }

  2593	    private fun focusMockSignInField(field: MockSignInField) {
  2594	        mockInputDimAwaitingField = field
  2595	        mockInputDimActiveField = field
  2596	        mockInputDimActiveDropdown = null
  2597	        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
  2598	        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
  2599	        if (focusedMockField != field) {
  2600	            activeComposingText = ""
  2601	            focusedMockField = field
  2602	            restartMockFormLabelAnimation()
  2603	            restartMockFormFocusAnimation()
  2604	        }
  2605	        requestFocus()
  2606	        post {
  2607	            val inputMethodManager = context.getSystemService(Context.INPUT_METHOD_SERVICE) as? InputMethodManager
  2608	            inputMethodManager?.restartInput(this)
  2609	            inputMethodManager?.showSoftInput(this, InputMethodManager.SHOW_IMPLICIT)
  2610	        }
  2611	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  2612	    }

  2614	    private fun clearMockSignInFieldFocus() {
  2615	        if (focusedMockField == null) return
  2616	        activeComposingText = ""
  2617	        focusedMockField = null
  2618	        mockInputDimAwaitingField = null
  2619	        restartMockFormLabelAnimation()
  2620	        restartMockFormFocusAnimation()
  2621	        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
  2622	        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
  2623	        val inputMethodManager = context.getSystemService(Context.INPUT_METHOD_SERVICE) as? InputMethodManager
  2624	        inputMethodManager?.hideSoftInputFromWindow(windowToken, 0)
  2625	        clearFocus()
  2626	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  2627	    }

  2685	    private fun mockActiveFieldX(field: MockSignInField): Float {
  2686	        if (activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword) return mockForgotPasswordFormX()
  2687	        if (activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) return mockFormX()
  2688	
  2689	        val middleColumnX = mockCreateFormX() + MOCK_FORM_WIDTH + MOCK_CREATE_FORM_COLUMN_GAP
  2690	        return when (field) {
  2691	            MockSignInField.Username,
  2692	            MockSignInField.Password,
  2693	            MockSignInField.ConfirmPassword -> mockCreateFormX()
  2694	            MockSignInField.Email -> middleColumnX
  2695	            MockSignInField.Month -> middleColumnX
  2696	            MockSignInField.Day -> middleColumnX + MOCK_DATE_MONTH_WIDTH + MOCK_DATE_FIELD_GAP
  2697	            MockSignInField.Year -> middleColumnX + MOCK_DATE_MONTH_WIDTH + MOCK_DATE_DAY_WIDTH + MOCK_DATE_FIELD_GAP * 2f
  2698	        }
  2699	    }
  2700	
  2701	    private fun mockActiveFieldY(field: MockSignInField, yOffset: Float, focusShiftY: Float): Float {
  2702	        if (activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword) {
  2703	            return mockForgotPasswordFieldY(yOffset, focusShiftY)
  2704	        }
  2705	
  2706	        val rowIndex = if (activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) {
  2707	            when (field) {
  2708	                MockSignInField.Username,
  2709	                MockSignInField.Email -> 0
  2710	                MockSignInField.Password -> 1
  2711	                MockSignInField.ConfirmPassword,
  2712	                MockSignInField.Month,
  2713	                MockSignInField.Day,
  2714	                MockSignInField.Year -> 2
  2715	            }
  2716	        } else {
  2717	            activeMockFields().indexOf(field).coerceAtLeast(0)
  2718	        }
  2719	        return MOCK_FORM_Y + yOffset + focusShiftY + rowIndex * (MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_GAP)
  2720	    }

  2725	            MockSignInField.Day -> MOCK_DATE_DAY_WIDTH
  2726	            MockSignInField.Year -> MOCK_DATE_YEAR_WIDTH
  2727	            else -> MOCK_FORM_WIDTH
  2728	        }
  2729	    }

  3088	    private fun mockLandscapeInputLiftTarget(): Float {
  3089	        if (width <= height) return 0f
  3090	        if (mockSignInStartMillis == null || isMockSignInClosing()) return 0f
  3091	        return if (
  3092	            focusedMockField != null ||
  3093	            expandedMockDropdown != null ||
  3094	            isSoftKeyboardVisible()
  3095	        ) {
  3096	            1f
  3097	        } else {
  3098	            0f
  3099	        }
  3100	    }
  3101	
  3102	    private fun mockLandscapeInputStageLiftY(stageScale: Float): Float {
  3103	        if (width <= height || mockSignInStartMillis == null) return 0f
  3104	        val liftMotion = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockLandscapeInputLiftProgress)
  3105	        return MOCK_FORM_LANDSCAPE_FOCUS_SHIFT_Y * stageScale * liftMotion
  3106	    }
  3107	
  3108	    private fun isSoftKeyboardVisible(): Boolean {
  3109	        val rootInsets = ViewCompat.getRootWindowInsets(this)
  3110	        if (rootInsets != null) {
  3111	            val imeInsets = rootInsets.getInsets(WindowInsetsCompat.Type.ime())
  3112	            if (rootInsets.isVisible(WindowInsetsCompat.Type.ime()) || imeInsets.bottom > 0) return true
  3113	        }
  3114	
  3115	        val rootHeight = rootView?.height ?: 0
  3116	        if (rootHeight <= 0) return false
  3117	        getWindowVisibleDisplayFrame(tempWindowRect)
  3118	        val obscuredHeight = rootHeight - tempWindowRect.height()
  3119	        return obscuredHeight > rootHeight * 0.18f
  3120	    }

  3227	    private fun appendMockSignInInput(rawInput: String): Boolean {
  3228	        val field = focusedMockField ?: return false
  3229	        val input = sanitizeMockSignInInput(rawInput)
  3230	        if (input.isEmpty()) return true
  3231	        val currentText = mockSignInFieldText(field)
  3232	        val baseText = currentText.dropLast(activeComposingText.length.coerceAtMost(currentText.length))
  3233	        val availableChars = (MOCK_FORM_INPUT_MAX_CHARS - baseText.length).coerceAtLeast(0)
  3234	        val insertedText = input.take(availableChars)
  3235	        setMockSignInFieldText(field, baseText + insertedText)
  3236	        activeComposingText = ""
  3237	        return true
  3238	    }
  3239	
  3240	    private fun replaceMockSignInComposingText(rawInput: String): Boolean {
  3241	        val field = focusedMockField ?: return false
  3242	        val input = sanitizeMockSignInInput(rawInput)
  3243	        val currentText = mockSignInFieldText(field)
  3244	        val baseText = currentText.dropLast(activeComposingText.length.coerceAtMost(currentText.length))
  3245	        val availableChars = (MOCK_FORM_INPUT_MAX_CHARS - baseText.length).coerceAtLeast(0)
  3246	        val composingText = input.take(availableChars)
  3247	        setMockSignInFieldText(field, baseText + composingText)
  3248	        activeComposingText = composingText
  3249	        return true
  3250	    }
  3251	
  3252	    private fun finishMockSignInComposingText(): Boolean {
  3253	        activeComposingText = ""
  3254	        return true
  3255	    }
  3256	
  3257	    private fun deleteMockSignInInput(beforeLength: Int): Boolean {
  3258	        val field = focusedMockField ?: return false
  3259	        val currentText = mockSignInFieldText(field)
  3260	        if (currentText.isEmpty()) {
  3261	            activeComposingText = ""
  3262	            return true
  3263	        }
  3264	        val deleteCount = beforeLength.coerceAtLeast(1).coerceAtMost(currentText.length)
  3265	        activeComposingText = ""
  3266	        setMockSignInFieldText(field, currentText.dropLast(deleteCount))
  3267	        return true
  3268	    }
  3269	
  3270	    private fun handleMockSignInEditorAction(actionCode: Int): Boolean {
  3271	        activeComposingText = ""
  3272	        val fields = activeMockFields()
  3273	        val fieldIndex = fields.indexOf(focusedMockField)
  3274	        if (fieldIndex >= 0 && fieldIndex < fields.lastIndex && actionCode != EditorInfo.IME_ACTION_DONE) {
  3275	            focusMockSignInField(fields[fieldIndex + 1])
  3276	        } else {
  3277	            val inputMethodManager = context.getSystemService(Context.INPUT_METHOD_SERVICE) as? InputMethodManager
  3278	            inputMethodManager?.hideSoftInputFromWindow(windowToken, 0)
  3279	            clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
  3280	        }
  3281	        return true
  3282	    }
  3283	
  3284	    private fun handleMockSignInKeyEvent(keyCode: Int, event: KeyEvent): Boolean {
  3285	        if (event.action != KeyEvent.ACTION_DOWN) return true
  3286	        return when (keyCode) {
  3287	            KeyEvent.KEYCODE_DEL -> deleteMockSignInInput(1)
  3288	            KeyEvent.KEYCODE_ENTER -> handleMockSignInEditorAction(EditorInfo.IME_ACTION_DONE)
  3289	            else -> {
  3290	                val typedCharacter = event.unicodeChar.takeIf { it != 0 }?.toChar()?.toString()
  3291	                typedCharacter?.let(::appendMockSignInInput) ?: false
  3292	            }
  3293	        }
  3294	    }

  3296	    private fun sanitizeMockSignInInput(input: String): String {
  3297	        return buildString {
  3298	            input.forEach { character ->
  3299	                if (character != '\n' && character != '\r' && !character.isISOControl()) {
  3300	                    append(character)
  3301	                }
  3302	            }
  3303	        }
  3304	    }
  3305	
  3306	    private fun trailingFittingText(text: String, maxWidth: Float, paint: Paint): String {
  3307	        if (paint.measureText(text) <= maxWidth) return text
  3308	
  3309	        var startIndex = 0
  3310	        while (startIndex < text.length && paint.measureText(text.substring(startIndex)) > maxWidth) {
  3311	            startIndex += 1
  3312	        }
  3313	        return text.substring(startIndex)
  3314	    }
  3315	
  3316	    private fun wrappedTextLines(text: String, maxWidth: Float, paint: Paint): List<String> {
  3317	        val lines = mutableListOf<String>()
  3318	        var currentLine = ""
  3319	        text.split(" ").forEach { word ->
  3320	            val candidateLine = if (currentLine.isEmpty()) word else "$currentLine $word"
  3321	            if (currentLine.isEmpty() || paint.measureText(candidateLine) <= maxWidth) {
  3322	                currentLine = candidateLine
  3323	            } else {
  3324	                lines += currentLine
  3325	                currentLine = word
  3326	            }
  3327	        }
  3328	        if (currentLine.isNotEmpty()) {
  3329	            lines += currentLine
  3330	        }
  3331	        return lines
  3332	    }
  3333	
  3334	    private fun moveToward(current: Float, target: Float, amount: Float): Float {
  3335	        return when {
  3336	            current < target -> (current + amount).coerceAtMost(target)
  3337	            current > target -> (current - amount).coerceAtLeast(target)
  3338	            else -> current
  3339	        }
  3340	    }

  3443	    private fun mockSignInProgress(): Float {
  3444	        val startMillis = mockSignInStartMillis ?: return 0f
  3445	        val elapsed = SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - startMillis
  3446	        val progress = (elapsed / mockSignInTransitionDurationMs().toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  3447	        return lerpFloat(
  3448	            mockSignInTransitionStartProgress,
  3449	            mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress,
  3450	            progress
  3451	        )
  3452	    }

  3457	    }

  3463	    private fun mockSignInTransitionDurationMs(): Long {
  3464	        val distance = abs(mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress - mockSignInTransitionStartProgress)

  3466	            .roundToInt()
  3467	            .coerceAtLeast(1)
  3468	            .toLong()
  3469	    }
  3470

  3472	        val startMillis = mockSignInStartMillis ?: return
  3473	        if (SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - startMillis < mockSignInTransitionDurationMs()) return
  3474	
  3475	        if (mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress == 0f) {
  3476	            mockSignInStartMillis = null
  3477	            activeMockFlow = null
  3478	            mockSignInTransitionStartProgress = 0f
  3479	            mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress = 0f
  3480	            pressedMockField = null
  3481	            pressedRememberMe = false
  3482	            pressedBackChevron = false
  3483	            pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
  3484	            pressedMockDropdown = null

  3486	            pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
  3487	            activeMockDropdownScroll = null
  3488	            mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
  3489	            mockDragReturnInProgress = false
  3490	            resetMockCreateAvatarCarousel()

  3492	        }

  3494	        mockSignInTransitionStartProgress = 1f
  3495	        mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress = 1f
  3496	    }

  3498	    private inner class MockSignInInputConnection : BaseInputConnection(this@CinerificIntroView, false) {

  3536	        override fun sendKeyEvent(event: KeyEvent): Boolean {
  3537	            if (event.action != KeyEvent.ACTION_DOWN) return true
  3538	            return when (event.keyCode) {
  3539	                KeyEvent.KEYCODE_DEL -> deleteMockSignInInput(1)
  3540	                KeyEvent.KEYCODE_ENTER -> handleMockSignInEditorAction(EditorInfo.IME_ACTION_DONE)
  3541	                else -> {

  3543	                    if (typedCharacter != null) {
  3544	                        appendMockSignInInput(typedCharacter)
  3545	                    } else {
  3546	                        super.sendKeyEvent(event)

  3657	private fun bootProgressAt(bootStartMillis: Long): Float {
  3658	    val elapsed = SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - bootStartMillis
  3659	    return (elapsed / BOOT_ANIMATION_MS.toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  3660	}
  3661	
  3662	private fun linearSegmentMs(progress: Float, startMs: Int, endMs: Int): Float {
  3663	    val ms = progress.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * BOOT_ANIMATION_MS
  3664	    return ((ms - startMs) / (endMs - startMs).toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  3665	}
  3666	
  3667	private fun easedSegmentMs(progress: Float, startMs: Int, endMs: Int): Float {
  3668	    return FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(linearSegmentMs(progress, startMs, endMs))
  3669	}
  3670	
  3671	private fun bouncySegmentMs(progress: Float, startMs: Int, endMs: Int): Float {
  3672	    return bouncyEasing(linearSegmentMs(progress, startMs, endMs))
  3673	}
  3674	
  3675	private fun bouncyEasing(value: Float): Float {
  3676	    val t = value.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  3677	    val base = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(t)
  3678	    val bounce = sin(t * PI.toFloat() * 4.5f) * (1f - t) * 0.13f
  3679	    return base + bounce
  3680	}
  3681	
  3682	private fun lerpFloat(start: Float, end: Float, amount: Float) = start + (end - start) * amount
  3683	
  3684	private fun lerpBounds(start: Bounds, end: Bounds, amount: Float) = Bounds(
  3685	    x = lerpFloat(start.x, end.x, amount),
  3686	    y = lerpFloat(start.y, end.y, amount),
  3687	    w = lerpFloat(start.w, end.w, amount),
  3688	    h = lerpFloat(start.h, end.h, amount)
  3689	)
  3690	
  3691	private fun offsetBounds(bounds: Bounds, yOffset: Float) = Bounds(
  3692	    x = bounds.x,
  3693	    y = bounds.y + yOffset,
  3694	    w = bounds.w,
  3695	    h = bounds.h
  3696	)
  3697	
  3698	private fun lerpColor(start: Int, end: Int, amount: Float): Int {
  3699	    val t = amount.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  3700	    return Color.argb(
  3701	        lerpInt(Color.alpha(start), Color.alpha(end), t),
  3702	        lerpInt(Color.red(start), Color.red(end), t),
  3703	        lerpInt(Color.green(start), Color.green(end), t),
  3704	        lerpInt(Color.blue(start), Color.blue(end), t)
  3705	    )
  3706	}
  3707	
  3708	private fun lerpInt(start: Int, end: Int, amount: Float): Int {
  3709	    return (start + (end - start) * amount).roundToInt()
  3710	}
  3711	
  3712	private fun combineBitmaps(base: Bitmap, overlay: Bitmap): Bitmap {
  3713	    val output = Bitmap.createBitmap(base.width, base.height, Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888)
  3714	    val canvas = Canvas(output)
  3715	    val paint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG or Paint.FILTER_BITMAP_FLAG or Paint.DITHER_FLAG)
  3716	    canvas.drawBitmap(base, 0f, 0f, paint)
  3717	    canvas.drawBitmap(overlay, 0f, 0f, paint)
  3718	    return output
  3719	}
  3720	
  3721	private fun circularBitmap(source: Bitmap): Bitmap {
  3722	    val output = Bitmap.createBitmap(source.width, source.height, Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888)
  3723	    val canvas = Canvas(output)
  3724	    val rect = RectF(0f, 0f, source.width.toFloat(), source.height.toFloat())
  3725	    val maskPaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG)
  3726	    val imagePaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG or Paint.FILTER_BITMAP_FLAG or Paint.DITHER_FLAG)
  3727	    canvas.drawOval(rect, maskPaint)
  3728	    imagePaint.xfermode = PorterDuffXfermode(PorterDuff.Mode.SRC_IN)
  3729	    canvas.drawBitmap(source, null, rect, imagePaint)
  3730	    imagePaint.xfermode = null
  3731	    return output
  3732	}

============================================================
FILE: README.md
============================================================

     1	# Cinerific
     2	
     3	Local Android project for the `prahlin/cinerific` GitHub repo.
     4	
     5	This is a first Kotlin / Jetpack Compose scaffold based on the Figma intro and sign-in frames. The current app opens as a tablet-friendly full-screen frame preview with:
     6	
     7	- Intro frame 1
     8	- Animated intro frame 2
     9	- Promo/logo intro frame 3
    10	- Sign-in/loading frame 4
    11	
    12	## Stack
    13	
    14	- Kotlin
    15	- Jetpack Compose Material 3
    16	- Android Gradle Plugin 8.5.x
    17	- Gradle 8.7
    18	
    19	## Run it
    20	
    21	Open the project in Android Studio and run `app`, or use the Gradle wrapper:
    22	
    23	```bash
    24	./gradlew :app:assembleDebug
    25	```
    26	
    27	The project pins Gradle to Android Studio's bundled JBR in `gradle.properties`, so command-line builds do not accidentally use an unsupported system JDK.
    28	
    29	From VS Code, run the default build task `Android: assemble debug`. There are also tasks for `Android: install debug` and `Android: launch Cinerific` when an emulator or device is connected.
    30	
    31	## Notes for the first Kotlin pass
    32	
    33	- The layout is intentionally simple and adaptive for tablets.
    34	- The current visuals are Compose approximations of the Figma frames and are meant to be replaced with production assets screen by screen.
    35	- The local project folder is the same path as the GitHub remote: `prahlin/cinerific`.

     4	}

     5	validateDistributionUrl=true
     6	zipStoreBase=GRADLE_USER_HOME
     7	zipStorePath=wrapper/dists


============================================================
FILE: read_project.sh
============================================================

     1	#!/bin/bash
     2	
     3	PROJECT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")" && pwd)"
     4	OUTPUT_FILE="$PROJECT_DIR/cinerific_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt"
     5	
     6	echo "Generating Cinerific project snapshot..."
     7	echo "Project directory: $PROJECT_DIR"
     8	echo "Snapshot: $OUTPUT_FILE"
     9	echo ""
    10	
    11	write_header() {
    12	  cat > "$OUTPUT_FILE" <<EOF
    13	Cinerific Android PROJECT SNAPSHOT
    14	Generated: $(date)
    15	Project directory: $PROJECT_DIR
    16	
    17	IMPORTANT INSTRUCTIONS FOR CHATGPT & CODEX:
    18	1. Read this entire snapshot before giving code edits.
    19	2. Preserve all assets, spacing, alignment, fonts, images, layout, animations, and relative positioning unless specifically asked to change them.
    20	3. Do not remove unrelated code.
    21	4. Do not rewrite large sections unless requested.
    22	5. When giving code changes, include exact line/row numbers whenever possible.
    23	6. Print full scripts when requested.
    24	7. Treat this project structure as the source of truth.
    25	8. If you don't understand something, ask for clarification instead of making assumptions.
    26	9. Warn me, or at least caution me, if I ask for something that would break the project or cause it to not work as intended.
    27	
    28	
    29	============================================================
    30	SNAPSHOT IGNORE POLICY
    31	============================================================
    32	
    33	This file is rebuilt from the current Cinerific workspace every time ./read_project.sh runs.
    34	Ignored paths are omitted from the project tree and file dumps unless they appear inside an included text file such as .gitignore or read_project.sh.
    35	
    36	The snapshot intentionally ignores:
    37	- .git
    38	- .gradle
    39	- .idea
    40	- app/build
    41	- build
    42	- .kotlin
    43	- .tmp
    44	- .cache
    45	- .DS_Store
    46	- local.properties
    47	- *_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt
    48	- *.jks
    49	- *.keystore
    50	
    51	Binary image and screenshot files are inventoried by path, size, and modified time only. Raw binary bytes are not printed.
    52	
    53	
    54	============================================================
    55	PROJECT FILE TREE
    56	============================================================
    57	
    58	EOF
    59	}
    60	
    61	print_file() {
    62	  local file="$1"
    63	
    64	  if [ -f "$file" ]; then
    65	    {
    66	      echo ""
    67	      echo ""
    68	      echo "============================================================"
    69	      echo "FILE: ${file#$PROJECT_DIR/}"
    70	      echo "============================================================"
    71	      echo ""
    72	      nl -ba "$file"
    73	    } >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
    74	  fi
    75	}
    76	
    77	print_binary_inventory() {
    78	  local title="$1"
    79	  local dir="$2"
    80	
    81	  {
    82	    echo ""
    83	    echo ""
    84	    echo "============================================================"
    85	    echo "$title"
    86	    echo "============================================================"
    87	    echo ""
    88	  } >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
    89	
    90	  if [ ! -d "$dir" ]; then
    91	    echo "Directory not present: ${dir#$PROJECT_DIR/}" >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
    92	    return
    93	  fi
    94	
    95	  find "$dir" -type f \
    96	    ! -name ".DS_Store" \
    97	    ! -name "*_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt" \
    98	    ! -name "*.jks" \
    99	    ! -name "*.keystore" \
   100	    | sort | while IFS= read -r file; do
   101	    local size
   102	    local modified
   103	    size="$(stat -f "%z" "$file" 2>/dev/null || stat -c "%s" "$file" 2>/dev/null || echo "unknown")"
   104	    modified="$(stat -f "%Sm" -t "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S" "$file" 2>/dev/null || stat -c "%y" "$file" 2>/dev/null || echo "unknown")"
   105	    printf '%s | %s bytes | modified %s\n' "${file#$PROJECT_DIR/}" "$size" "$modified" >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   106	  done
   107	}
   108	
   109	write_snapshot() {
   110	  write_header
   111	
   112	  find "$PROJECT_DIR" \
   113	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.git" -prune -o \
   114	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.gradle" -prune -o \
   115	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.idea" -prune -o \
   116	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/app/build" -prune -o \
   117	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/build" -prune -o \
   118	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.kotlin" -prune -o \
   119	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.tmp" -prune -o \
   120	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.cache" -prune -o \
   121	    -name ".DS_Store" -prune -o \
   122	    -name "local.properties" -prune -o \
   123	    -name "*_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt" -prune -o \
   124	    -name "*.jks" -prune -o \
   125	    -name "*.keystore" -prune -o \
   126	    -print | sort >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   127	
   128	  cat >> "$OUTPUT_FILE" <<EOF
   129	
   130	
   131	============================================================
   132	FILE CONTENTS — ANDROID SOURCE
   133	============================================================
   134	
   135	EOF
   136	
   137	  for file in \
   138	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml" \
   139	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/java/com/prahlin/cinerific/MainActivity.kt" \
   140	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/java/com/prahlin/cinerific/ui/CinerificApp.kt" \
   141	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/java/com/prahlin/cinerific/ui/CinerificIntroView.kt" \
   142	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/java/com/prahlin/cinerific/ui/theme/Theme.kt" \
   143	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/values/strings.xml" \
   144	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/values/themes.xml" \
   145	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/values-night/themes.xml" \
   146	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/values-v31/themes.xml" \
   147	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/drawable/splash_transparent.xml" \
   148	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/drawable/ic_launcher_background.xml" \
   149	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/drawable/ic_launcher_foreground.xml" \
   150	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/mipmap-anydpi-v26/ic_launcher.xml" \
   151	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/mipmap-anydpi-v26/ic_launcher_round.xml"
   152	  do
   153	    print_file "$file"
   154	  done
   155	
   156	  cat >> "$OUTPUT_FILE" <<EOF
   157	
   158	
   159	============================================================
   160	FILE CONTENTS — ROOT / TOOLING
   161	============================================================
   162	
   163	EOF
   164	
   165	  for file in \
   166	    "$PROJECT_DIR/.gitignore" \
   167	    "$PROJECT_DIR/.github/copilot-instructions.md" \
   168	    "$PROJECT_DIR/.vscode/mcp.json" \
   169	    "$PROJECT_DIR/.vscode/tasks.json" \
   170	    "$PROJECT_DIR/README.md" \
   171	    "$PROJECT_DIR/build.gradle.kts" \
   172	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/build.gradle.kts" \
   173	    "$PROJECT_DIR/settings.gradle.kts" \
   174	    "$PROJECT_DIR/gradle.properties" \
   175	    "$PROJECT_DIR/gradle/wrapper/gradle-wrapper.properties" \
   176	    "$PROJECT_DIR/read_project.sh"
   177	  do
   178	    print_file "$file"
   179	  done
   180	
   181	  print_binary_inventory "BINARY / IMAGE ASSET INVENTORY — DRAWABLE-NODPI" "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/drawable-nodpi"
   182	  print_binary_inventory "BINARY / SCREENSHOT INVENTORY" "$PROJECT_DIR/screenshots"
   183	
   184	  cat >> "$OUTPUT_FILE" <<EOF
   185	
   186	
   187	============================================================
   188	NOT INCLUDED IN SNAPSHOT
   189	============================================================
   190	
   191	The Cinerific snapshot intentionally excludes generated/cache/runtime/private directories:
   192	- .git
   193	- .gradle
   194	- .idea
   195	- app/build
   196	- build
   197	- .kotlin
   198	- .tmp
   199	- .cache
   200	- .DS_Store
   201	- local.properties
   202	- *_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt
   203	- *.jks
   204	- *.keystore
   205	
   206	The snapshot lists binary assets and screenshots by path, size, and modified time, but does not print raw PNG/JPG/PDF bytes.
   207	
   208	EOF
   209	}
   210	
   211	write_snapshot
   212	
   213	echo "Done."
   214	echo ""
   215	echo "Snapshot created here:"
   216	echo "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   217	echo ""
   218	echo "Open it with:"
   219	echo "open \"$OUTPUT_FILE\""

============================================================
NOT INCLUDED IN SNAPSHOT
============================================================

The Cinerific snapshot intentionally excludes generated/cache/runtime/private directories:
- .git
- .gradle
- .idea
- app/build
- build
- .kotlin
- .tmp
- .cache
- .DS_Store
- local.properties
- *_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt
- *.jks
- *.keystore

The snapshot lists binary assets and screenshots by path, size, and modified time, but does not print raw PNG/JPG/PDF bytes.
